<p>Recently, I've become interested in anytime-valid inference, especially e-values. I think the ideas are cool and useful, both in theory and in practice, so I'm planning to learn more about them whenever I have some spare time (mostly for fun).</p>&#10;<h2 id="user-content-1-revisiting-hypothesis-testing">1. Revisiting Hypothesis Testing</h2>&#10;<p>For some of the concepts in mathematical statistics, I usually refer to Statistical Inference by George Casella and Roger Berger. In sequential testing, we collect data until we have enough <em>evidence</em> to make a decision.</p>&#10;<section class="academic-block academic-definition"><p class="academic-heading">Definition 3 Testing by betting</h2>&#10;<p>So far, we have seen that an e-process gives us an anytime-valid test. The next question is, how can we construct an e-process..?</p>&#10;<p>One way is to think of hypothesis testing as a betting game.</p>&#10;<p>Suppose that a bettor starts with an initial wealth <span class="katex"><mjx-assistive-mml class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msub><mi>W</mi><mn>0</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">W_{0}=1</annotation></semantics></math></mjx-assistive-mml><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="katex-base"><span class="katex-strut" style="height:0.8333em;vertical-align:-0.15em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1389em;">W</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.3011em;"><span style="top:-2.55em;margin-left:-0.1389em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="katex-sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.15em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="katex-base"><span class="katex-strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>. At each time <span class="katex"><mjx-assistive-mml class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><annotation
